<div class="bsky-card" markdown="1">

<div class="bsky-header" markdown="1">

<div class="bsky-content" markdown="1">

There's a lot of drama in the Speedrun community and I'm curious could atproto be used for speedrunning. As I undersetand it atproto is a stream of data coming in that anyone can consume so why not broadcast your PB or PR and use whichever view you like







<a href="https://www.reddit.com/r/speedrun/comments/1x3hh46/srcs_terms_of_use_and_privacy_policy_may_violate/" class="bsky-link-preview-wrapper" target="_blank">
<div class="bsky-link-preview" markdown="1">

<div class="link-info" markdown="1">
